# Populated places in Canada: feature class P, codes PPL/PPLA*/PPLC/PPLL. That is real inhabited
# places and not neighbourhoods of cities (PPLX would put a dozen Toronto districts in as though they
# were towns) and not places that have stopped existing (PPLH/PPLQ/PPLW).
#
# ASCII spellings, because us-cities.txt is pure ASCII and the board is typed on a US keyboard.
# Geo.Normalise folds accents too, so Montreal and Montréal both find this row.
#
# Where one name occurs twice in a province the larger is kept: that is the one somebody typing the
# name means. A "Greater X" gets a bare "X" alias as well, unless X is already a different place
# in that province -- the census says Greater Sudbury and everybody else, the game included, says
# Sudbury.
#
# Same format as us-cities.txt so one loader reads both. Province codes match MapCoverage, which is
# what the board and the Where you run setting are keyed on.
